[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU),
module name `tpu`, implementing the EXACT per-cycle behavior of the logikbench
`tpu` benchmark (zeroasiccorp/logikbench, MIT).

FUNCTION: computes C = A * B tile products on an 8x8 grid of processing
elements. B (the "weight" matrix, 8-bit signed) is loaded into the array and
held; A (the "activation" matrix, 8-bit signed) streams through; result rows
C (32-bit signed) drain out the bottom.

PROTOCOL:
  1. Weight load: assert w_valid for 8 cycles; w_data carries one weight row
     per cycle, 8 signed bytes packed { col 7 ... col 0 }, BOTTOM matrix row
     first (cycle k carries B[7-k][*]; the weights shift south). After 8
     cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
  2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle (packed the
     same way). Row m of the result, C[m][*] packed { col 7 ... col 0 } as 8
     signed 32-bit words, appears on c_data with c_valid high exactly 15
     (= 2*8-1) compute cycles after its A row was accepted.
  - The array advances only while w_valid is low: asserting w_valid stalls
    any in-flight computation (and reloads weights). Zeros are injected while
    a_valid is low, so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
  - Multiple A tiles may stream back-to-back against the same loaded weights.

CYCLE ACCURACY: c_valid is compared against the original design EVERY cycle;
c_data is compared on every cycle where c_valid is high. The 15-cycle
compute-latency and the stall semantics are part of the contract.

Ports:
  clk, rst (async active-high reset, asserted only before the stream begins)
  w_valid, w_data[63:0]
  a_valid, a_data[63:0]
  c_valid, c_data[255:0]

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` is the
original logikbench RTL (tpu_pe / tpu_array / tpu: PE mesh, per-row input
skew, per-column output de-skew, valid pipeline). It passes every cycle of the golden
trace in `vectors.dat` (directed zero/identity/extreme tiles, random tiles,
back-to-back streams, a_valid gaps, mid-drain weight reloads, random soak).
Study it, then optimize while preserving the cycle-accurate behavior. The
sibling benchmark `tpu_spire` shares the identical testbench and vectors
with a SpireHDL starting point.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 1/60]
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Processing element (PE) for the weight-stationary systolic array (TPU MXU).
//
// Each PE holds one stationary signed weight 'w'. Two independent datapaths
// share the cell:
//
//   Weight load (ld): the weights form a per-column shift register that loads
//   from the top. On each ld cycle the PE captures the weight from the PE above
//   (w_north) and presents its stored weight downward (w_south). After N load
//   cycles every PE in a column holds its final stationary weight.
//
//   Compute (en): the activation flows west to east (registered, one hop per
//   cycle) and the partial sum flows north to south, accumulating the local
//   product a_west * w. This is the only arithmetic cell in the array: one
//   signed DW x DW multiply plus an ACCW-bit add.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)
   (
    input		  clk,
    input		  rst,	      // synchronous, active high
    input		  ld,	      // weight-load shift enable
    input		  en,	      // compute enable
    input [DW-1:0]	  w_north,    // weight in from PE above
    input [DW-1:0]	  a_west,     // activation in from west
    input [ACCW-1:0]	  psum_north, // partial sum in from above
    output [DW-1:0]	  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below
    output reg [DW-1:0]	  a_east,     // activation out to east
    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below
    );

   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain
   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   assign w_south = w;

   // local product: the ONLY signed operation. Operands are interpreted as
   // signed here via $signed; the 2*DW-bit result holds the two's-complement
   // product, which is then sign-extended to the accumulator width below.
   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);

   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst) begin
         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};
         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};
         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
      end
      else begin
         if (ld)
           w <= w_north;
         if (en) begin
            a_east     <= a_west;
            psum_south <= psum_north +
                          {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};
         end
      end
   end

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).
//
// One tpu_pe per grid element, wired as a 2D mesh (per CLAUDE.md: replicated
// hardware via generate, never a procedural unroll):
//
//   - weights load from the top: w_top[j] feeds column j; each column is a
//     vertical shift register (w_south of PE(i,j) -> w_north of PE(i+1,j)).
//   - activations flow west to east: a_left[i] enters row i; a_east of PE(i,j)
//     feeds a_west of PE(i,j+1).
//   - partial sums flow north to south and accumulate; the top row's psum_north
//     is tied to zero (fresh accumulation), and c_bot[j] is the column-j result
//     leaving the bottom row.
//
// With weight B[i][j] held at PE(i,j) and activation A[m][i] entering row i,
// column j computes C[m][j] = sum_i A[m][i] * B[i][j]. Input/output skew (which
// time-aligns rows and columns) lives in the tpu top, not here.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_array
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N PEs)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	// synchronous, active high
    input		ld,	// weight-load shift enable
    input		en,	// compute enable
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_top,	// weight inputs, one per column (load)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_left,	// activation inputs, one per row
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_bot	// column results, one per column
    );

   // mesh nets: a_h carries activations east, psum_v carries sums south,
   // w_v carries weights south during load.
   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];
   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];

   genvar	   i, j;
   generate
      // west edge: activation inputs into column 0 of each row
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west
         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];
      end
      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out

      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge

         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];
         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];
      end
      // the PE mesh
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row
         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col
            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe

               .rst        (rst),

               .en         (en),

               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),

               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),
               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),
               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));
         end
      end
   endgenerate

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).
//
// Computes one tile product C = A * B, where A and B are N x N signed operands
// (DW bits) and C is N x N signed (ACCW bits). B is the "weight" matrix, loaded
// into and held by the array; A is the "activation" matrix, streamed through; C
// drains out the bottom. Built from a tpu_array of N x N tpu_pe cells.
//
// Operation has two phases the host drives via valids:
//
//   1. Weight load: assert w_valid for N cycles. w_data carries one weight row
//      per cycle, N signed weights packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }. Because the
//      array loads top-down (shift south), push the BOTTOM matrix row first:
//      cycle k carries B[N-1-k][*]. After N cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
//
//   2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle, a_data
//      packed { col N-1 ... col 0 } = A[m][N-1..0]. Row m of the result, C[m][*]
//      (packed the same way), appears on c_data with c_valid high, 2*N-1 cycles
//      after its A row was accepted.
//
// Time alignment: row i of A is delayed i cycles entering the array (input
// skew) so partial sums chain correctly down each column; column j of the
// result is delayed N-1-j cycles (output de-skew) so a full result row leaves
// together. Both skew networks and the valid pipeline advance only with the
// array (compute enable), so the 2*N-1 latency is exact. Per CLAUDE.md the skew
// chains are generated per-row/column shift registers, not a procedural unroll.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	 // synchronous, active high
    input		w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_data,	 // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    input		a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_data,	 // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    output		c_valid, // result-row valid
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_data	 // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    );

   // total injection-to-output latency, in compute cycles
   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;

   // load weights while w_valid; otherwise the array computes (and drains)
   wire	      ld = w_valid;
   wire	      en = ~w_valid;

   //##########################################################################
   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles. Zero is injected
   // when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;
   genvar	   i;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew
         wire [DW-1:0] din =
		       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};
         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;
         end
         else begin : g_skewn
            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  sr[0] <= din;
                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // The systolic array.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;
   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array
     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),
      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));

   //##########################################################################
   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j cycles so all columns of a
   // result row line up. Column N-1 (longest path) needs no delay.
   //##########################################################################
   genvar j;
   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew
         localparam D = N - 1 - j;
         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];
         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;
         end
         else begin : g_deskewn
            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  dr[0] <= cin;
                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.
   //##########################################################################
   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst)
        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};
      else if (en)
        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};
   end
   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];

endmodule

[Step 2/60]
// Self-checking testbench for the tpu benchmark (8x8 weight-stationary
// systolic matmul tile). Reads vectors.dat, one line per clock cycle:
//   w_valid w_data(64b) a_valid a_data(64b)   c_valid c_data(256b)   (hex)
// The expected outputs are the ORIGINAL logikbench tpu's per-cycle behavior;
// c_valid and c_data are compared CYCLE-ACCURATELY every cycle (c_data only
// while c_valid is high — the original holds stale data between results).
module tb;
  localparam N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic clk, rst;
  logic w_valid, a_valid, c_valid;
  logic [N*DW-1:0] w_data, a_data;
  logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data;

  tpu dut
    (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .w_valid(w_valid), .w_data(w_data),
     .a_valid(a_valid), .a_data(a_data), .c_valid(c_valid), .c_data(c_data));

  initial clk = 0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  integer fd, rc, line_num;
  string line_buf;
  logic s_wv, s_av, e_cv;
  logic [63:0] s_wd, s_ad;
  logic [255:0] e_cd;

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    w_valid = 0; a_valid = 0; w_data = 0; a_data = 0;
    rst = 1;
    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;

    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    line_num = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      line_num = line_num + 1;
      void'($fgets(line_buf, fd));
      if (line_buf.len() == 0) continue;
      if (line_buf.substr(0, 0) == "#") continue;
      rc = $sscanf(line_buf, "%h %h %h %h %h %h", s_wv, s_wd, s_av, s_ad, e_cv, e_cd);
      if (rc != 6) continue;
      w_valid = s_wv; w_data = s_wd; a_valid = s_av; a_data = s_ad;
      @(negedge clk);
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      if (c_valid !== e_cv || (e_cv && c_data !== e_cd)) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR line=%0d exp(cv=%b cd=%h) got(cv=%b cd=%h)",
                 line_num, e_cv, e_cd, c_valid, c_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
        if (total_errors > 20) begin
          $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
          $fatal(1, "FAIL (too many errors, aborting)");
        end
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule


[ASSISTANT]
I'll analyze the starting point first, then design an optimized architecture.

Key insights from studying the starting point:
1. The 32-bit accumulators are overkill: 8 products of 8-bit signed values fit in 19 bits (max |sum| = 8×16384 = 131072).
2. The output de-skew network (896 FFs) can be **eliminated entirely**: if every column's activation is delayed by exactly 7 cycles (instead of column j delayed by j), all columns finish simultaneously at exactly the +15 cycle c_valid timing.
3. Per-row skew + horizontal delay can be merged into a single per-row delay line of depth `i+7`.

Let me check the vectors and create the first design.
  -> read_file(filename='vectors.dat', limit=30)
  -> create_file(filename='tpu_v1.v', content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU), 8x8.\n//\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\n//\n// Architecture notes (vs. the original per-PE systolic mesh):\n//  * Partial sums are held at exactly PW = 2*DW + clog2(N) = 19 bits: the sum\n//    of N signed DWxDW products lies in [-N*2^(2DW-2), N*2^(2DW-2)] which\n//    always fits (N*2^14 = 2^17 <= 2^18-1 ... the extreme +131072 needs the\n//    19th bit).  Results are sign-extended to ACCW at the output only.\n//  * Column j of the original array finishes (8 + j) cycles after its A row\n//    enters, so the original needs a per-column de-skew of (N-1-j) cycles.\n//    Here instead every column's activation is delayed by exactly N-1 = 7\n//    cycles, so ALL columns finish simultaneously at cycle 2N-1 = 15 and the\n//    de-skew network disappears completely.\n//  * Row i's activation byte is therefore delayed by (i + N-1) compute\n//    cycles: a single shift line per row (products stay staggered by one\n//    cycle per row, which is what the south-flowing partial-sum chain needs).\n//  * The valid pipe delays a_valid by LAT = 2N-1 compute cycles; everything\n//    (delay lines, accumulators, valid pipe) stalls while w_valid is high.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t      clk,\n    input\t\t      rst,     // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t      w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t      w_data,  // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\t      a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t      a_data,  // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\t      c_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t      c_data   // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   // exact partial-sum width: N signed 2*DW-bit products\n   localparam PW = 2*DW + $clog2(N);\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   // the array advances (and the weight registers shift) on disjoint phases\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Activation distribution: row i carries byte i of a_data delayed by\n   // (i + N-1) compute cycles; zeros are injected while a_valid is low so\n   // idle/drain cycles contribute nothing to the accumulators.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [DW-1:0] arow [0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   gi, gj;\n   generate\n      for (gi = 0; gi < N; gi = gi + 1) begin : g_aline\n\t localparam DEPTH = gi + N - 1;\n\t reg [DW-1:0] sh [0:DEPTH-1];\n\t integer\t    k;\n\t wire [DW-1:0]\t    din = a_valid ? a_data[gi*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n\t always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t    if (rst) begin\n\t       for (k = 0; k < DEPTH; k = k + 1)\n\t\t sh[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t    end\n\t    else if (en) begin\n\t       sh[0] <= din;\n\t       for (k = 1; k < DEPTH; k = k + 1)\n\t\t sh[k] <= sh[k-1];\n\t    end\n\t end\n\t assign arow[gi] = sh[DEPTH-1];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The PE mesh: stationary weight per cell, product accumulated into the\n   // south-flowing partial-sum chain.  Row 0's chain input is hardwired zero.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [DW-1:0]\t  wreg [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n   reg [PW-1:0]\t  psum [0:N-1][0:N-1];\n\n   generate\n      for (gi = 0; gi < N; gi = gi + 1) begin : g_row\n\t for (gj = 0; gj < N; gj = gj + 1) begin : g_col\n\t    wire [DW-1:0] w_north =\n\t\t\t  (gi == 0) ? w_data[gj*DW +: DW] : wreg[gi-1][gj];\n\t    wire [PW-1:0] p_north =\n\t\t\t  (gi == 0) ? {PW{1'b0}} : psum[gi-1][gj];\n\t    wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(arow[gi]) * $signed(wreg[gi][gj]);\n\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (rst)\n\t\t wreg[gi][gj] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t       else if (w_valid)\n\t\t wreg[gi][gj] <= w_north;\n\t    end\n\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (rst)\n\t\t psum[gi][gj] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n\t       else if (en)\n\t\t psum[gi][gj] <= p_north +\n\t\t\t\t {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\t    end\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Outputs: bottom-row partial sums are already complete results.\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (gj = 0; gj < N; gj = gj + 1) begin : g_out\n\t assign c_data[gj*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t   {{(ACCW-PW){psum[N-1][gj][PW-1]}}, psum[N-1][gj]};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n\tvpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n\tvpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
Now I understand the key issue! Let me analyze more carefully.

**Critical insight**: The original's per-PE schedule is: product `A[m][i]*B[i][j]` computes at compute-edge `E(m)+i+j` (row skew `i` + **horizontal east-flow** `j`). The weight version each product sees depends on `i+j`, so mid-drain weight reloads cut each column's accumulation at a **different** anti-diagonal. My v1 collapsed this to `E(m)+i+7` (j-independent), producing different hybrid values. The exact schedule (vertical skew + horizontal chain + de-skew) is mandatory.

So the safe optimization is: **keep the exact schedule, shrink the accumulators** from 32 to 19 bits (8 products × 16384 max = 131072 = 2^17 needs 19 bits signed).
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU), 8x8.\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\n//\n// Schedule (identical to the original, required for cycle accuracy):\n//   product A[m][i]*B[i][j] is formed at compute edge E(m)+i+j, where E(m) is\n//   the compute edge that accepted activation row m.  Row i's activation is\n//   delayed i cycles (input skew) and then flows east one hop per cycle; the\n//   partial sums flow south; column j's finished result is delayed 7-j cycles\n//   (output de-skew) so the whole result row leaves together, 2N-1 = 15\n//   compute cycles after its A row was accepted.\n//\n// Optimization vs. the original: the accumulators are PW = 2*DW + clog2(N) =\n// 19 bits instead of 32.  Every partial sum is a sum of at most N signed\n// DWxDW products, |.| <= N*2^(2DW-2) = 131072 = 2^17, which fits in 19 signed\n// bits, so the 19-bit datapath is exact; results are sign-extended to 32 bits\n// at the outputs only.  This shrinks 64 accumulator registers/adders and the\n// 28 de-skew registers with zero behavior change.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t      clk,\n    input\t\t      rst,\t// synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t      ld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t      en,\t// compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t      w_north,\t// weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t      a_west,\t// activation in from west\n    input [PW-1:0]\t      psum_north,// partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t      w_south,\t// stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t      a_east,\t// activation out to east\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t      psum_south // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t w\t   <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t a_east\t   <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t psum_south <= {PW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n\t if (ld)\n\t   w <= w_north;\n\t if (en) begin\n\t    a_east\t   <= a_west;\n\t    psum_south <= psum_north +\n\t\t\t  {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\t end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t      clk,\n    input\t\t      rst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\t      w_valid,\t // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t      w_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\t      a_valid,\t // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\t      a_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\t      c_valid,\t // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\t      c_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam PW  = 2*DW + $clog2(N);\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles.  Zero is\n   // injected while a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n\t wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n\t if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_skewn\n\t    reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n\t    integer\t    k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (rst) begin\n\t\t  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t       end\n\t       else if (en) begin\n\t\t  sr[0] <= din;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n\n   genvar\t   gi, gj;\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [PW-1:0]\t  psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]\t  w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   generate\n      for (gi = 0; gi < N; gi = gi + 1) begin : g_west\n\t assign a_h[gi][0] = a_left[gi*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (gj = 0; gj < N; gj = gj + 1) begin : g_edge\n\t assign psum_v[0][gj] = {PW{1'b0}};\n\t assign w_v[0][gj]    = w_data[gj*DW +: DW];\n\t assign c_bot[gj*PW +: PW] = psum_v[N][gj];\n      end\n      for (gi = 0; gi < N; gi = gi + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n\t for (gj = 0; gj < N; gj = gj + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n\t    tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n\t      (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(w_valid), .en(en),\n\t       .w_north(w_v[gi][gj]), .a_west(a_h[gi][gj]),\n\t       .psum_north(psum_v[gi][gj]),\n\t       .w_south(w_v[gi+1][gj]), .a_east(a_h[gi][gj+1]),\n\t       .psum_south(psum_v[gi+1][gj]));\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j compute cycles so all columns\n   // of a result row line up.  Values are sign-extended to ACCW here.\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (gj = 0; gj < N; gj = gj + 1) begin : g_deskew\n\t localparam D = N - 1 - gj;\n\t wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[gj*PW +: PW];\n\t if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n\t    assign c_data[gj*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_deskewn\n\t    reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n\t    integer\t     k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (rst) begin\n\t\t  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n\t       end\n\t       else if (en) begin\n\t\t  dr[0] <= cin;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign c_data[gj*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n\tvpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n\tvpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='tpu_v2.v')

[ASSISTANT]
v2 passes with cost 3.32M. Now the big optimization: the 831ps critical path is the per-PE multiply+accumulate. My plan: a **fused radix-4 Booth + carry-save (CSA) tree with a redundant carry-save accumulator** — this removes the 19-bit carry-propagate adder from the per-cycle path entirely (the CPA moves to the output side, once per column).
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU), 8x8.\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\n//\n// Schedule (identical to the original, required for cycle accuracy):\n//   product A[m][i]*B[i][j] is formed at compute edge E(m)+i+j (row skew i,\n//   horizontal east flow j), partial sums flow south, column j's result is\n//   de-skewed by N-1-j cycles so the whole result row leaves together,\n//   2N-1 = 15 compute cycles after its A row was accepted.\n//\n// Datapath (v3):\n//   * Partial sums are exact at PW = 2*DW + clog2(N) = 19 signed bits\n//     (|sum of 8 products| <= 8*2^14), results sign-extended to 32b at the\n//     outputs only.\n//   * Each PE accumulates in REDUNDANT carry-save form: two registers\n//     (sum, carry).  The per-cycle path is Booth-4 encoding of the stationary\n//     weight (4 partial products) compressed together with the incoming\n//     (sum, carry) pair by a 4-level 3:2 compressor tree - no carry-propagate\n//     adder in the loop.  The single CPA per column happens once, on the\n//     output side (bottom row -> de-skew), off the accumulate critical path.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_cs\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // accumulator width (signed, exact)\n   (\n    input wire\t\t clk,\n    input wire\t\t rst,\t// synchronous, active high\n    input wire\t\t ld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input wire\t\t en,\t// compute enable\n    input wire [DW-1:0]\t w_north,\n    input wire [DW-1:0]\t a_west,\n    input wire [PW-1:0]\t ps_north_s, // partial sum (carry-save sum row)\n    input wire [PW-2:0]\t ps_north_c, // partial sum (carry row, bit0 = 0)\n    output wire [DW-1:0] w_south,\n    output reg  [DW-1:0] a_east,\n    output reg  [PW-1:0] ps_south_s,\n    output reg  [PW-2:0] ps_south_c\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   //#########################################################################\n   // Radix-4 Booth recoding of the stationary weight w:\n   //   digit k = -2*w[2k+1] + w[2k] + w[2k-1]  (k = 0..3, w[-1] = 0)\n   // with B0 = w[2k-1], B1 = w[2k], B2 = w[2k+1]:\n   //   |d| = 1 iff B0^B1, |d| = 2 iff ~（B0^B1) & (B1^B2), d = 0 otherwise,\n   //   sign(d) = B2.\n   //#########################################################################\n   wire x1_0 = w[0];\n   wire x2_0 = w[0] ^ w[1];\n   wire ng_0 = w[1];\n   wire x1_1 = w[1] ^ w[2];\n   wire x2_1 = w[2] ^ w[3];\n   wire ng_1 = w[3];\n   wire x1_2 = w[3] ^ w[4];\n   wire x2_2 = w[4] ^ w[5];\n   wire ng_2 = w[5];\n   wire x1_3 = w[5] ^ w[6];\n   wire x2_3 = w[6] ^ w[7];\n   wire ng_3 = w[7];\n\n   wire [8:0] mag0 = x1_0 ? {a_west[7], a_west} :\n\t\t     (x2_0 ? {a_west, 1'b0} : 9'b0);\n   wire [8:0] mag1 = x1_1 ? {a_west[7], a_west} :\n\t\t     (x2_1 ? {a_west, 1'b0} : 9'b0);\n   wire [8:0] mag2 = x1_2 ? {a_west[7], a_west} :\n\t\t     (x2_2 ? {a_west, 1'b0} : 9'b0);\n   wire [8:0] mag3 = x1_3 ? {a_west[7], a_west} :\n\t\t     (x2_3 ? {a_west, 1'b0} : 9'b0);\n\n   wire [8:0] pp0 = ng_0 ? ~mag0 : mag0;\n   wire [8:0] pp1 = ng_1 ? ~mag1 : mag1;\n   wire [8:0] pp2 = ng_2 ? ~mag2 : mag2;\n   wire [8:0] pp3 = ng_3 ? ~mag3 : mag3;\n\n   // partial-product rows, sign-extended to PW bits, offsets 0/2/4/6\n   wire [PW-1:0] r0 = {{(PW-9){pp0[8]}}, pp0};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r1 = {{(PW-11){pp1[8]}}, pp1, 2'b00};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r2 = {{(PW-13){pp2[8]}}, pp2, 4'b0000};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r3 = {{(PW-15){pp3[8]}}, pp3, 6'b000000};\n   // incoming partial sum, carry-save form\n   wire [PW-1:0] r4 = ps_north_s;\n   wire [PW-1:0] r5 = {ps_north_c, 1'b0};\n   // negative-digit carry-in bits (positions 0,2,4,6 - mutually disjoint)\n   wire [PW-1:0] r6 = {{(PW-7){1'b0}}, ng_3, 1'b0, ng_2, 1'b0, ng_1, 1'b0, ng_0};\n\n   //#########################################################################\n   // 4-level 3:2 compressor tree: 7 rows -> 2 rows (mod 2^PW)\n   //#########################################################################\n   wire [PW-1:0] g1 = (r0 & r1) | (r2 & (r0 | r1));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s1 = r0 ^ r1 ^ r2;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c1 = {g1[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] g2 = (r3 & r4) | (r5 & (r3 | r4));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s2 = r3 ^ r4 ^ r5;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c2 = {g2[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] g3 = (s1 & c1) | (s2 & (s1 | c1));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s3 = s1 ^ c1 ^ s2;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c3 = {g3[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] g4 = (s3 & c3) | (c2 & (s3 | c3));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s4 = s3 ^ c3 ^ c2;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c4 = {g4[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] g5 = (s4 & c4) | (r6 & (s4 | c4));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s5 = s4 ^ c4 ^ r6;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c5 = {g5[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t w\t   <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t a_east\t   <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t ps_south_s <= {PW{1'b0}};\n\t ps_south_c <= {(PW-1){1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n\t if (ld)\n\t   w <= w_north;\n\t if (en) begin\n\t    a_east\t   <= a_west;\n\t    ps_south_s <= s5;\n\t    ps_south_c <= c5[PW-1:1];\n\t end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result width (signed)\n   (\n    input wire\t\t clk,\n    input wire\t\t rst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input wire\t\t w_valid,// weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input wire [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data, // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input wire\t\t a_valid,// activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input wire [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data, // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output wire\t\t c_valid,// result-row valid\n    output wire [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data  // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam PW  = 2*DW + $clog2(N);\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles.  Zero is\n   // injected while a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n\t wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n\t if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_skewn\n\t    reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n\t    integer\t    k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (rst) begin\n\t\t  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t       end\n\t       else if (en) begin\n\t\t  sr[0] <= din;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array: carry-save partial sums flow south.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [PW-1:0]  ps_s [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [PW-2:0]  ps_c [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h  [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  w_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [PW-1:0]  res  [0:N-1];\n\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n\t assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n\t assign ps_s[0][j] = {PW{1'b0}};\n\t assign ps_c[0][j] = {(PW-1){1'b0}};\n\t assign w_v[0][j]  = w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n\t // the only carry-propagate add per column: on the output side\n\t assign res[j] = ps_s[N][j] + {ps_c[N][j], 1'b0};\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n\t for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n\t    tpu_pe_cs #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n\t      (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(w_valid), .en(en),\n\t       .w_north(w_v[i][j]), .a_west(a_h[i][j]),\n\t       .ps_north_s(ps_s[i][j]), .ps_north_c(ps_c[i][j]),\n\t       .w_south(w_v[i+1][j]), .a_east(a_h[i][j+1]),\n\t       .ps_south_s(ps_s[i+1][j]), .ps_south_c(ps_c[i+1][j]));\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j compute cycles so all columns\n   // of a result row line up.  Values are sign-extended to ACCW here.\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n\t localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n\t if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){res[j][PW-1]}}, res[j]};\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_deskewn\n\t    reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n\t    integer\t     k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (rst) begin\n\t\t  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n\t       end\n\t       else if (en) begin\n\t\t  dr[0] <= res[j];\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n\tvpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n\tvpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='tpu_v3.v')

[ASSISTANT]
Excellent — v3 passes with cost 2.49M (delay 594ps, down from 831ps). Now the big win: the Booth encoding (x1/x2/ng bits) depends **only on the stationary weight**, so it can be *stored in registers* and shifted down the column during load — removing the entire encode+mux chain (~280ps) from the per-cycle critical path. Each PE then computes partial products as fast 2-level `((x1&a)|(x2&a2)) ^ ng` terms.
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU), 8x8.\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\n//\n// Schedule (identical to the original, required for cycle accuracy):\n//   product A[m][i]*B[i][j] is formed at compute edge E(m)+i+j (row skew i,\n//   horizontal east flow j), partial sums flow south, column j's result is\n//   de-skewed by N-1-j cycles so the whole result row leaves together,\n//   2N-1 = 15 compute cycles after its A row was accepted.\n//\n// Datapath (v4):\n//   * Partial sums are exact at PW = 2*DW + clog2(N) = 19 signed bits\n//     (|sum of 8 products| <= 8*2^14), results sign-extended to 32b at the\n//     outputs only.\n//   * Each PE accumulates in REDUNDANT carry-save form (sum, carry) - the\n//     per-cycle path is a 4-level 3:2 compressor tree with NO carry-propagate\n//     adder in the loop; the single CPA per column sits on the output side.\n//   * The Booth-4 recoding of the stationary weight depends only on the\n//     weight, so each PE stores the 12 recoding bits (x1/x2/ng per digit)\n//     and the LOAD CHAIN shifts recodings, not raw weights.  The recode\n//     logic exists only at the top of each column.  The per-cycle path is:\n//     activation FF -> 2-level partial-product select -> 4 CSA levels -> FF.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_cs2\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // accumulator width (signed, exact)\n   (\n    input wire\t\t clk,\n    input wire\t\t rst,\t// synchronous, active high\n    input wire\t\t ld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input wire\t\t en,\t// compute enable\n    input wire [11:0]\t enc_north, // Booth recoding in from PE above\n    input wire [DW-1:0]\t a_west,\n    input wire [PW-1:0]\t ps_north_s, // partial sum (carry-save sum row)\n    input wire [PW-2:0]\t ps_north_c, // partial sum (carry row, bit0 = 0)\n    output wire [11:0]\t enc_south,\n    output reg  [DW-1:0] a_east,\n    output reg  [PW-1:0] ps_south_s,\n    output reg  [PW-2:0] ps_south_c\n    );\n\n   //#########################################################################\n   // Stored radix-4 Booth recoding of the stationary weight w:\n   //   digit k = -2*w[2k+1] + w[2k] + w[2k-1]  (k = 0..3, w[-1] = 0)\n   // x1_k = |d|=1, x2_k = |d|=2 (mutually exclusive), ng_k = d negative.\n   // With M = x1_k ? a : (x2_k ? 2a : 0) (9-bit, sign-extended):\n   //   contribution = ng ? (~M + 1) : M   (the +1 rides the recoding row)\n   // so each partial-product bit is simply M_b ^ ng_k.\n   //#########################################################################\n   reg [11:0] enc;\n   assign enc_south = enc;\n\n   wire [3:0] x1 = enc[11:8];\n   wire [3:0] x2 = enc[7:4];\n   wire [3:0] ng = enc[3:0];\n\n   wire [DW-1:0] a2 = {a_west[DW-2:0], 1'b0}; // 2a, low bit unused\n\n   // per-digit 9-bit magnitude-select rows: m = (x1 & a) | (x2 & 2a)\n   function [8:0] mrow;\n      input [3:0] xs1, xs2;\n      input [DW-1:0] av;\n      begin\n\t mrow[0] = xs1 & av[0];\n\t mrow[1] = (xs1 & av[1]) | (xs2 & av[0]);\n\t mrow[2] = (xs1 & av[2]) | (xs2 & av[1]);\n\t mrow[3] = (xs1 & av[3]) | (xs2 & av[2]);\n\t mrow[4] = (xs1 & av[4]) | (xs2 & av[3]);\n\t mrow[5] = (xs1 & av[5]) | (xs2 & av[4]);\n\t mrow[6] = (xs1 & av[6]) | (xs2 & av[5]);\n\t mrow[7] = (xs1 & av[7]) | (xs2 & av[6]);\n\t mrow[8] = (xs1 | xs2) & av[7];\t // 2a's top bit = sign bit\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [8:0] m0 = mrow(x1[0] ? 4'hF : 4'h0, x2[0] ? 4'hF : 4'h0, a_west);\n   wire [8:0] m1 = mrow(x1[1] ? 4'hF : 4'h0, x2[1] ? 4'hF : 4'h0, a_west);\n   wire [8:0] m2 = mrow(x1[2] ? 4'hF : 4'h0, x2[2] ? 4'hF : 4'h0, a_west);\n   wire [8:0] m3 = mrow(x1[3] ? 4'hF : 4'h0, x2[3] ? 4'hF : 4'h0, a_west);\n\n   wire [8:0] p0 = m0 ^ {9{ng[0]}};\n   wire [8:0] p1 = m1 ^ {9{ng[1]}};\n   wire [8:0] p2 = m2 ^ {9{ng[2]}};\n   wire [8:0] p3 = m3 ^ {9{ng[3]}};\n\n   // partial-product rows, sign-extended to PW bits, offsets 0/2/4/6\n   wire [PW-1:0] r0 = {{(PW-9){p0[8]}}, p0};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r1 = {{(PW-11){p1[8]}}, p1, 2'b00};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r2 = {{(PW-13){p2[8]}}, p2, 4'b0000};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r3 = {{(PW-15){p3[8]}}, p3, 6'b000000};\n   // incoming partial sum, carry-save form\n   wire [PW-1:0] r4 = ps_north_s;\n   wire [PW-1:0] r5 = {ps_north_c, 1'b0};\n   // negative-digit carry-in bits (positions 0,2,4,6 - mutually disjoint)\n   wire [PW-1:0] r6 = {{(PW-7){1'b0}}, ng[3], 1'b0, ng[2], 1'b0, ng[1], 1'b0, ng[0]};\n\n   //#########################################################################\n   // 4-level 3:2 compressor tree: 7 rows -> 2 rows (mod 2^PW)\n   //#########################################################################\n   wire [PW-1:0] g1 = (r0 & r1) | (r2 & (r0 | r1));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s1 = r0 ^ r1 ^ r2;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c1 = {g1[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] g2 = (r3 & r4) | (r5 & (r3 | r4));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s2 = r3 ^ r4 ^ r5;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c2 = {g2[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] g3 = (s1 & c1) | (s2 & (s1 | c1));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s3 = s1 ^ c1 ^ s2;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c3 = {g3[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] g4 = (s3 & c3) | (c2 & (s3 | c3));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s4 = s3 ^ c3 ^ c2;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c4 = {g4[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] g5 = (s4 & c4) | (r6 & (s4 | c4));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s5 = s4 ^ c4 ^ r6;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c5 = {g5[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t enc\t   <= 12'b0;\n\t a_east\t   <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t ps_south_s <= {PW{1'b0}};\n\t ps_south_c <= {(PW-1){1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n\t if (ld)\n\t   enc <= enc_north;\n\t if (en) begin\n\t    a_east\t   <= a_west;\n\t    ps_south_s <= s5;\n\t    ps_south_c <= c5[PW-1:1];\n\t end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result width (signed)\n   (\n    input wire\t\t clk,\n    input wire\t\t rst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input wire\t\t w_valid,// weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input wire [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data, // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input wire\t\t a_valid,// activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input wire [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data, // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output wire\t\t c_valid,// result-row valid\n    output wire [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data  // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam PW  = 2*DW + $clog2(N);\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Booth recoding of a raw weight byte (used only at the column tops).\n   //##########################################################################\n   function [11:0] booth;\n      input [DW-1:0] w;\n      reg [3:0] b0, b1, b2;\n      integer   k;\n      begin\n\t for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin\n\t    b0 = (k == 0) ? 4'd0 : {4{w[2*k-1]}};\n\t    b1 = {4{w[2*k]}};\n\t    b2 = {4{w[2*k+1]}};\n\t    booth[11-k] = b0[0] ^ b1[0];\t // x1_k = |d| = 1\n\t    booth[7-k]  = b1[0] ^ b2[0];\t // x2_k = |d| = 2\n\t    booth[3-k]  = b2[0];\t\t // ng_k = d negative\n\t end\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles.  Zero is\n   // injected while a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n\t wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n\t if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_skewn\n\t    reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n\t    integer\t    k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (rst) begin\n\t\t  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t       end\n\t       else if (en) begin\n\t\t  sr[0] <= din;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array: carry-save partial sums flow south, Booth recodings\n   // shift south during load.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [PW-1:0]  ps_s [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [PW-2:0]  ps_c [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [11:0]\t  enc_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h  [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [PW-1:0]  res  [0:N-1];\n\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n\t assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n\t assign ps_s[0][j]  = {PW{1'b0}};\n\t assign ps_c[0][j]  = {(PW-1){1'b0}};\n\t assign enc_v[0][j] = booth(w_data[j*DW +: DW]);\n\t // the only carry-propagate add per column: on the output side\n\t assign res[j] = ps_s[N][j] + {ps_c[N][j], 1'b0};\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n\t for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n\t    tpu_pe_cs2 #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n\t      (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(w_valid), .en(en),\n\t       .enc_north(enc_v[i][j]), .a_west(a_h[i][j]),\n\t       .ps_north_s(ps_s[i][j]), .ps_north_c(ps_c[i][j]),\n\t       .enc_south(enc_v[i+1][j]), .a_east(a_h[i][j+1]),\n\t       .ps_south_s(ps_s[i+1][j]), .ps_south_c(ps_c[i+1][j]));\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j compute cycles so all columns\n   // of a result row line up.  Values are sign-extended to ACCW here.\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n\t localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n\t if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){res[j][PW-1]}}, res[j]};\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_deskewn\n\t    reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n\t    integer\t     k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (rst) begin\n\t\t  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n\t       end\n\t       else if (en) begin\n\t\t  dr[0] <= res[j];\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n\tvpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n\tvpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='tpu_v4.v')

[ASSISTANT]
v4 passes at 2.15M (delay 533ps). The critical path is the Booth magnitude-select (~250ps — the `4'hF`-mux idiom synthesized poorly) plus the 4-level CSA tree. Let me rewrite the select as clean per-bit AND/OR logic:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU), 8x8.\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\n//\n// Schedule (identical to the original, required for cycle accuracy):\n//   product A[m][i]*B[i][j] is formed at compute edge E(m)+i+j (row skew i,\n//   horizontal east flow j), partial sums flow south, column j's result is\n//   de-skewed by N-1-j cycles so the whole result row leaves together,\n//   2N-1 = 15 compute cycles after its A row was accepted.\n//\n// Datapath (v5):\n//   * Partial sums are exact at PW = 2*DW + clog2(N) = 19 signed bits\n//     (|sum of 8 products| <= 8*2^14), results sign-extended to 32b at the\n//     outputs only.\n//   * Each PE accumulates in REDUNDANT carry-save form (sum, carry) - the\n//     per-cycle path is a 4-level 3:2 compressor tree with NO carry-propagate\n//     adder in the loop; the single CPA per column sits on the output side.\n//   * The Booth-4 recoding of the stationary weight depends only on the\n//     weight, so each PE stores the 12 recoding bits (x1/x2/ng per digit)\n//     and the LOAD CHAIN shifts recodings, not raw weights.  The per-cycle\n//     magnitude select is a plain 2-AND + OR per bit:\n//         m[b] = (x1 & a[b]) | (x2 & a[b-1])\n//     followed by the ng inversion mask, then the compressor tree.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_cs2\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // accumulator width (signed, exact)\n   (\n    input wire\t\t clk,\n    input wire\t\t rst,\t// synchronous, active high\n    input wire\t\t ld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input wire\t\t en,\t// compute enable\n    input wire [11:0]\t enc_north, // Booth recoding in from PE above\n    input wire [DW-1:0]\t a_west,\n    input wire [PW-1:0]\t ps_north_s, // partial sum (carry-save sum row)\n    input wire [PW-2:0]\t ps_north_c, // partial sum (carry row, bit0 = 0)\n    output wire [11:0]\t enc_south,\n    output reg  [DW-1:0] a_east,\n    output reg  [PW-1:0] ps_south_s,\n    output reg  [PW-2:0] ps_south_c\n    );\n\n   //#########################################################################\n   // Stored radix-4 Booth recoding of the stationary weight w:\n   //   digit k = -2*w[2k+1] + w[2k] + w[2k-1]  (k = 0..3, w[-1] = 0)\n   // x1_k = |d|=1, x2_k = |d|=2 (mutually exclusive), ng_k = d negative.\n   //#########################################################################\n   reg [11:0] enc;\n   assign enc_south = enc;\n\n   wire [3:0] x1 = enc[11:8];\n   wire [3:0] x2 = enc[7:4];\n   wire [3:0] ng = enc[3:0];\n\n   // sign-extended a and 2a (9-bit rows)\n   wire [8:0] ae  = {a_west[DW-1], a_west};\n   wire [8:0] a2e = {a_west[DW-1], a_west[DW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   // per-digit magnitude row: m = (x1 & a) | (x2 & 2a);  p = m ^ ng mask\n   wire [8:0] p0 = (({9{x1[0]}} & ae) | ({9{x2[0]}} & a2e)) ^ {9{ng[0]}};\n   wire [8:0] p1 = (({9{x1[1]}} & ae) | ({9{x2[1]}} & a2e)) ^ {9{ng[1]}};\n   wire [8:0] p2 = (({9{x1[2]}} & ae) | ({9{x2[2]}} & a2e)) ^ {9{ng[2]}};\n   wire [8:0] p3 = (({9{x1[3]}} & ae) | ({9{x2[3]}} & a2e)) ^ {9{ng[3]}};\n\n   // partial-product rows, sign-extended to PW bits, offsets 0/2/4/6\n   wire [PW-1:0] r0 = {{(PW-9){p0[8]}}, p0};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r1 = {{(PW-11){p1[8]}}, p1, 2'b00};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r2 = {{(PW-13){p2[8]}}, p2, 4'b0000};\n   wire [PW-1:0] r3 = {{(PW-15){p3[8]}}, p3, 6'b000000};\n   // incoming partial sum, carry-save form\n   wire [PW-1:0] r4 = ps_north_s;\n   wire [PW-1:0] r5 = {ps_north_c, 1'b0};\n   // negative-digit carry-in bits (positions 0,2,4,6 - mutually disjoint)\n   wire [PW-1:0] r6 = {{(PW-7){1'b0}}, ng[3], 1'b0, ng[2], 1'b0, ng[1], 1'b0, ng[0]};\n\n   //#########################################################################\n   // 4-level 3:2 compressor tree: 7 rows -> 2 rows (mod 2^PW)\n   //#########################################################################\n   wire [PW-1:0] g1 = (r0 & r1) | (r2 & (r0 | r1));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s1 = r0 ^ r1 ^ r2;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c1 = {g1[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] g2 = (r3 & r4) | (r5 & (r3 | r4));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s2 = r3 ^ r4 ^ r5;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c2 = {g2[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] g3 = (s1 & c1) | (s2 & (s1 | c1));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s3 = s1 ^ c1 ^ s2;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c3 = {g3[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] g4 = (s3 & c3) | (c2 & (s3 | c3));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s4 = s3 ^ c3 ^ c2;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c4 = {g4[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] g5 = (s4 & c4) | (r6 & (s4 | c4));\n   wire [PW-1:0] s5 = s4 ^ c4 ^ r6;\n   wire [PW-1:0] c5 = {g5[PW-2:0], 1'b0};\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n\t enc\t   <= 12'b0;\n\t a_east\t   <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t ps_south_s <= {PW{1'b0}};\n\t ps_south_c <= {(PW-1){1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n\t if (ld)\n\t   enc <= enc_north;\n\t if (en) begin\n\t    a_east\t   <= a_west;\n\t    ps_south_s <= s5;\n\t    ps_south_c <= c5[PW-1:1];\n\t end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result width (signed)\n   (\n    input wire\t\t clk,\n    input wire\t\t rst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input wire\t\t w_valid,// weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input wire [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data, // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input wire\t\t a_valid,// activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input wire [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data, // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output wire\t\t c_valid,// result-row valid\n    output wire [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data  // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam PW  = 2*DW + $clog2(N);\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Booth recoding of a raw weight byte (used only at the column tops).\n   //##########################################################################\n   function [11:0] booth;\n      input [DW-1:0] w;\n      reg [3:0] b0, b1, b2;\n      integer   k;\n      begin\n\t for (k = 0; k < 4; k = k + 1) begin\n\t    b0 = (k == 0) ? 4'd0 : {4{w[2*k-1]}};\n\t    b1 = {4{w[2*k]}};\n\t    b2 = {4{w[2*k+1]}};\n\t    booth[8+k]  = b0[0] ^ b1[0];\t // x1_k = |d| = 1\n\t    booth[4+k]  = ~(b0[0] ^ b1[0]) & (b1[0] ^ b2[0]); // x2_k = |d| = 2\n\t    booth[k]    = b2[0];\t\t // ng_k = d negative\n\t end\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i compute cycles.  Zero is\n   // injected while a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n\t wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n\t if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_skewn\n\t    reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n\t    integer\t    k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (rst) begin\n\t\t  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n\t       end\n\t       else if (en) begin\n\t\t  sr[0] <= din;\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // The systolic array: carry-save partial sums flow south, Booth recodings\n   // shift south during load.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [PW-1:0]  ps_s [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [PW-2:0]  ps_c [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [11:0]\t  enc_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h  [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [PW-1:0]  res  [0:N-1];\n\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n\t assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n\t assign ps_s[0][j]  = {PW{1'b0}};\n\t assign ps_c[0][j]  = {(PW-1){1'b0}};\n\t assign enc_v[0][j] = booth(w_data[j*DW +: DW]);\n\t // the only carry-propagate add per column: on the output side\n\t assign res[j] = ps_s[N][j] + {ps_c[N][j], 1'b0};\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n\t for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n\t    tpu_pe_cs2 #(.DW(DW), .PW(PW)) u_pe\n\t      (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(w_valid), .en(en),\n\t       .enc_north(enc_v[i][j]), .a_west(a_h[i][j]),\n\t       .ps_north_s(ps_s[i][j]), .ps_north_c(ps_c[i][j]),\n\t       .enc_south(enc_v[i+1][j]), .a_east(a_h[i][j+1]),\n\t       .ps_south_s(ps_s[i+1][j]), .ps_south_c(ps_c[i+1][j]));\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j compute cycles so all columns\n   // of a result row line up.  Values are sign-extended to ACCW here.\n   //##########################################################################\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n\t localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n\t if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){res[j][PW-1]}}, res[j]};\n\t end\n\t else begin : g_deskewn\n\t    reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n\t    integer\t     k;\n\t    always @(posedge clk) begin\n\t       if (rst) begin\n\t\t  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    dr[k] <= {PW{1'b0}};\n\t       end\n\t       else if (en) begin\n\t\t  dr[0] <= res[j];\n\t\t  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n\t\t    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n\t       end\n\t    end\n\t    assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t      {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n\t end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n\tvpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n\tvpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='tpu_v5.v')

v5 passes at 2.124M. Now the next big step: move the Booth recoding to the **activation** side (computed per-cycle from `a_west`, 1 XOR level) so the per-PE select becomes a single AOI22 gate against **stationary weight bits** — and the 12-bit recode shift chain (768 FFs) disappears entirely:
  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (TPU-style MXU), 8x8.\n// Cycle-accurate re-implementation of the logikbench `tpu` benchmark.\n//\n// Schedule (identical to the original, required for cycle accuracy):\n//   product A[m][i]*B[i][j] is formed at compute edge E(m)+i+j (row skew i,\n//   horizontal east flow j), partial sums flow south, column j's result is\n//   de-skewed by N-1-j cycles so the whole result row leaves together,\n//   2N-1 = 15 compute cycles after its A row was accepted.\n//\n// Datapath (v6):\n//   * Partial sums are exact at PW = 2*DW + clog2(N) = 19 signed bits\n//     (|sum of 8 products| <= 8*2^14), results sign-extended to 32b at the\n//     outputs only.\n//   * Each PE accumulates in REDUNDANT carry-save form (sum, carry) - the\n//     per-cycle path is a 4-level 3:2 compressor tree with NO carry-propagate\n//     adder in the loop; the single CPA per column sits on the output side.\n//   * Booth-4 recoding is applied to the FLOWING activation (a_west): the\n//     recode bits x1/x2/ng are 1-2 gate levels from a_west, and the partial\n//     product select runs against the STATIONARY weight register bits:\n//         m_k[b] = (x1_k & w[2k+b]) | (x2_k & w[2k+b-1]),  p_k = m_k ^ ng_k\n//     i.e. one AOI22-style gate per bit.
